    "description": "Geometric Quantum Machine Learning (GQML) applies quantum computing to machine learning by leveraging symmetries and structures in data.     "description": "This repository contains code for implementing quantum pattern recognition using swap tests and destructive swap tests on IBM's quantum processors.
